{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"pikes","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A very long spear used two-handed by infantry soldiers for thrusting (not throwing), both for attacks on enemy foot soldiers and as a countermeasure against cavalry assaults."],"tags":["historical"],"topics":["government","military","politics","war"]},{"glosses":["A sharp, pointed staff or implement."]},{"glosses":["A large haycock (“conical stack of hay left in a field to dry before adding to a haystack”)."]},{"glosses":["Any carnivorous freshwater fish of the genus Esox, especially the northern pike, Esox lucius."]},{"glosses":["A position with the knees straight and a tight bend at the hips with the torso folded over the legs, usually part of a jack-knife."],"topics":["diving","gymnastics","hobbies","lifestyle","sports"]},{"glosses":["A pointy extrusion at the toe of a shoe."],"tags":["dated"],"topics":["fashion","lifestyle"]},{"glosses":["A pointy extrusion at the toe of a shoe.","A style of shoes with pikes, popular in Europe in the 14th and 15th centuries."],"tags":["dated","historical"],"topics":["fashion","lifestyle"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Scafell Pike is the highest mountain in England.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Especially in place names: a hill or mountain, particularly one with a sharp peak or summit."],"tags":["Northern-England"]},{"glosses":["A pick, a pickaxe."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["A hayfork."],"tags":["UK","dialectal","obsolete"]},{"glosses":["A penis."],"tags":["euphemistic","obsolete","often"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/paɪk/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"the fish","word":"ged"},{"sense":"the fish species Esox lucius","word":"northern pike"}],"upstream_line":297191,"upstream_offset":1485006342,"upstream_record_sha256":"09864ecc61d53232d55dfa36f58300a354d1095352a63ca55b814c7c69d9eb8c","word":"pike"}
